- **rt-idor**: Red-team assessment of insecure-direct-object-reference and broken-object-level-authorization boundaries: numeric/UUID/encoded object IDs, mass-assignment, reference leakage, GraphQL node IDs, gRPC/protobuf, cloud-object URLs and OpenTelemetry leaks. Re-expressed as scoped cross-account experiments using owned test accounts and a bounded ID set with negative controls instead of mass enumeration of real users. ([annatchijova/SKILLS](https://github.com/annatchijova/SKILLS/tree/HEAD/red-team/rt-idor) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **rt-improve**: Simplify existing code, improve performance, and refine UI motion while preserving functional behavior. Use when explicitly asked to apply rt-improve to a page, component, module, or flow; the focus is cleanup and refinement, not new features or redesign. ([ayv4zyan/skills](https://github.com/ayv4zyan/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/rt-improve))
- **rt-initial-access**: Red-team assessment of initial-access exposure in an authorized engagement: phishing/social delivery, public-facing exploitation, valid-account abuse, exposed services and supply-chain entry. Re-expressed as a scoped exposure-and-detection exercise that maps and tests entry vectors against owned/consented assets with a benign marker and detection measurement, not a live intrusion or mass phishing playbook. ([annatchijova/SKILLS](https://github.com/annatchijova/SKILLS/tree/HEAD/red-team/rt-initial-access) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **rt-interactsh**: Callback evidence review: evidence-driven review of supplied source, configuration and isolated local lab fixtures. ([PhanNhatTien090/TYAN](https://github.com/PhanNhatTien090/TYAN/tree/HEAD/skills/rt-interactsh) · ★1)
- **rt-ios-dynamic-frida**: Instrumented iOS lab review: evidence-driven review of supplied source, configuration and isolated local lab fixtures. ([PhanNhatTien090/TYAN](https://github.com/PhanNhatTien090/TYAN/tree/HEAD/skills/rt-ios-dynamic-frida) · ★1)
- **rt-ios-redteam-pipeline**: iOS application review: evidence-driven review of supplied source, configuration and isolated local lab fixtures. ([PhanNhatTien090/TYAN](https://github.com/PhanNhatTien090/TYAN/tree/HEAD/skills/rt-ios-redteam-pipeline) · ★1)
- **rt-iot**: Red-team assessment of IoT/embedded device boundaries: firmware extraction and analysis, hardware debug interfaces (UART/JTAG/SWD/SPI flash), bootloader and secure-boot, protocol and update-integrity, and credential/key storage. Re-expressed as scoped experiments on an owned device with lab bench access that prove one boundary weakness against the device's own firmware with hardened-config controls instead of attacks on deployed third-party devices. ([annatchijova/SKILLS](https://github.com/annatchijova/SKILLS/tree/HEAD/red-team/rt-iot) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **rt-journal-match**: Use when an author asks "which journal should I send this to?" or needs the best resubmission target after a reject. Profiles the paper, shortlists candidates from an index of 743 venues with tools/match_venues.py, and ranks them into reach / match / safe with a resubmission ladder. Reads live venue facts from each pack's source-map; defers fit judgment to the venue's own topic-selection skill. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Research-Toolkit-Skills/skills/rt-journal-match) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **rt-journey-activations**: Configure RT journey activations - webhooks, Salesforce, email, and other integrations for real-time event-triggered actions ([treasure-data/td-skills](https://github.com/treasure-data/td-skills/tree/HEAD/realtime-skills/rt-journey-activations) · ★22)
- **rt-journey-create**: Create RT 2.0 Journeys (event-triggered) that activate when specific events occur, sending real-time data to external systems ([treasure-data/td-skills](https://github.com/treasure-data/td-skills/tree/HEAD/realtime-skills/rt-journey-create) · ★22)
- **rt-journey-monitor**: Monitor RT journeys - track activation success, debug failures, query activation logs, and optimize performance ([treasure-data/td-skills](https://github.com/treasure-data/td-skills/tree/HEAD/realtime-skills/rt-journey-monitor) · ★22)
- **rt-k8s-attacks**: Red-team assessment of Kubernetes cluster authority boundaries: service-account token discovery and RBAC abuse, kubelet/API-server reachability, privileged/hostPath/hostPID pod specs and escape, secrets access, and lateral movement across namespaces. Re-expressed as scoped experiments in an owned cluster using can-i permission mapping, a planted namespace/secret and RBAC-hardened negatives instead of live cluster takeover. ([annatchijova/SKILLS](https://github.com/annatchijova/SKILLS/tree/HEAD/red-team/rt-k8s-attacks) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **rt-keylogger-arch**: Blue-team assessment of keylogging / input-capture detection and defense: understanding the capture mechanisms (kernel/user hooks, raw input, accessibility APIs, hardware) so defenders can detect them, and measuring whether endpoint controls catch an authorized emulation. Re-expressed as a detection-and-prevention exercise on an owned lab host with a benign input-capture stub and PASS/WARN/FAIL scoring, not an implementation guide for covert surveillance. ([annatchijova/SKILLS](https://github.com/annatchijova/SKILLS/tree/HEAD/red-team/rt-keylogger-arch) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **rt-krack-fragattacks**: Red-team assessment of 802.11 protocol-flaw patch status: KRACK (key-reinstallation in the 4-way/group-key handshake) and FragAttacks (fragmentation/aggregation design and implementation flaws). Re-expressed as a scoped patch-verification experiment in an isolated RF lab against owned AP/client that confirms whether the stack is vulnerable or patched, with a patched-stack control, instead of attacks on live networks. ([annatchijova/SKILLS](https://github.com/annatchijova/SKILLS/tree/HEAD/red-team/rt-krack-fragattacks) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **rt-ladder-ev**: Use when choosing between submission sequences rather than between single venues — "should I try the top journal first, or start one rung down?", or when a tenure/job-market clock makes time-to-print the binding constraint. Costs a resubmission ladder in months and in probability of ever placing, using each venue's own turnaround and desk-reject figures. Follows rt-journal-match, which produces the ladder this one prices. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Research-Toolkit-Skills/skills/rt-ladder-ev) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **rt-lateral-movement**: Red-team assessment of lateral-movement boundaries in an authorized engagement: credential reuse/pass-the-hash/pass-the-ticket, remote-execution services (SMB/WMI/WinRM/SSH/RDP), trust and segmentation traversal, and session/credential harvesting. Re-expressed as scoped experiments across owned/planted hosts that prove one hop with a benign marker, measure detection, and respect segmentation controls instead of free-roaming a live network. ([annatchijova/SKILLS](https://github.com/annatchijova/SKILLS/tree/HEAD/red-team/rt-lateral-movement) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **rt-levels**: Embody Pieter Levels (levelsio) — solo founder, indie hacker, ship-fast evangelist. Use for solo business ideas, bootstrapping validation, product launches, indie hacking strategy, or when the user needs someone to cut through overthinking and just ship. ([risingdream/roundtable](https://github.com/risingdream/roundtable/tree/HEAD/skills/builders/rt-levels) · ★29)
- **rt-library-construction-workflow-analysis**: Use when building an in-house RT/m/z metabolite standard library from authentic-standard LC-MS mzML files, or when benchmarking that library-construction effort (per-standard time, total minutes, fold reduction) against a manual peak-picking baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/rt-library-construction-workflow-analysis))
